Imported references retain their recorded publishers and original publication/access dates. Source terms were not recorded in this inventory; no open license is inferred. Full source objects, including archived URLs, are in source-records.json.

Imported coordinate provenance:
Each original location.note is retained verbatim. A location citation can support the named place while the coordinate was obtained separately; the first listed geometry reference is not necessarily the coordinate provider. Original geocoder responses and lookup hashes were not included in this inventory.
Where a location note identifies OpenStreetMap or its Nominatim geocoder as the coordinate provider: © OpenStreetMap contributors; ODbL 1.0, https://www.openstreetmap.org/copyright . This attribution concerns the derived reference point, not the separately cited company or news page.
Where a location note identifies Wikipedia: coordinate provenance is credited to Wikipedia contributors; article URLs are retained when the original record supplied them. Coordinate-specific source terms and underlying coordinate provenance were not recorded, and no license is inferred for the separately cited sources.
Imported evidence content_hash values hash the canonical factual source-record projection specified in consolidation.py, not a downloaded publisher page or geocoder response.
